I’ll skip breakfast and lunch without really meaning to, and then even at dinner I just kind of pick at food, even when my wife’s cooked. My jeans are starting to feel loose, so I figured I should bring it up.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling down or depressed lately?\n\nPatient: Yeah… most days lately I’ve been waking up with this heavy, kind of flat feeling.
